select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1762 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='6fa74885-fd1c-41f1-8264-1444bcd43a40-1776603810' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='6fa74885-fd1c-41f1-8264-1444bcd43a40-1776603810' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '6fa74885-fd1c-41f1-8264-1444bcd43a40-1776603810' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select * from es_sale where rfid='{61D6BB47}'
 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'f999443e-a09f-4c92-8c69-165f6c186e13-1773434135' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{61D6BB47}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1627 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='f999443e-a09f-4c92-8c69-165f6c186e13-1773434135' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='f999443e-a09f-4c92-8c69-165f6c186e13-1773434135' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'f999443e-a09f-4c92-8c69-165f6c186e13-1773434135' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=99 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select is_vip, is_free_time, guid, id, rfid, name, dob, email, phone, balance_main, balance_bonus, card_sno, created_at, total_token from es_customer where is_new=1 and rfid='{3968FBE2}'
select ifnull(max(created_at_unix), 0) as uts from es_billing where rfid='{3968FBE2}'
select * from es_billing where rfid='{3968FBE2}' and guid='582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542'
select * from es_softplay_customer_slots where rfid='{3968FBE2}' and customer_id='99'
select * from es_sale where rfid='{3968FBE2}'
 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{3968FBE2}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=99 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select ei.id, CONCAT('Token: ' , ei.token_count, ', Balance Qty: ', (ei.qty - get_used_item_qty(ei.id)) , ' , Item: ',  ei.name) AS n from es_item as ei WHERE ei.qty > 0 and ei.token_count > 0  order by ei.token_count, ei.name

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{3968FBE2}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=99 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '582fa089-74ac-473c-b223-20e481008e54-1760098542' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='c9123451-46fd-4e7c-a0c2-1eebb8181359-1776619418' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'c9123451-46fd-4e7c-a0c2-1eebb8181359-1776619418' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{573E2202}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1763 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='c9123451-46fd-4e7c-a0c2-1eebb8181359-1776619418' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='c9123451-46fd-4e7c-a0c2-1eebb8181359-1776619418' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'c9123451-46fd-4e7c-a0c2-1eebb8181359-1776619418' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1764 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='0fb9328a-35cd-4f8c-bb05-c1ef9beb25b7-1776628188' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='0fb9328a-35cd-4f8c-bb05-c1ef9beb25b7-1776628188' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc

select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='0fb9328a-35cd-4f8c-bb05-c1ef9beb25b7-1776628188' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '039c3303-b036-4f36-8384-f8ca7b21a881-1776628584' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{56A010AF}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1765 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='039c3303-b036-4f36-8384-f8ca7b21a881-1776628584' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='039c3303-b036-4f36-8384-f8ca7b21a881-1776628584' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '039c3303-b036-4f36-8384-f8ca7b21a881-1776628584' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;
